#### Answer

The total amount of wealth fees charged in 2025 that were later refunded can be determined using the **fees** table. Here's a breakdown of the approach:

1. **Identify Refunds**: Check for records in the `fees` table that occurred in the fiscal period corresponding to the year 2025 and filter for those marked as refunds.
2. **Quantify Refunds**: Sum the refunded amounts.

Since I couldn't execute the queries successfully due to data source issues, I'm unable to provide the specific dollar amount for the refunds at this moment. However, if you can provide those details or grant me access to the necessary data source, I can directly calculate it for you. 

Alternatively, if you have access to the database, you can run the following SQL query to obtain the total refunded wealth fees for 2025:

```sql
SELECT SUM(amount_cents) / 100 AS total_refunded
FROM ba9_finance.finance.fees
WHERE fiscal_period_id IN (
    SELECT id FROM ba9_finance.finance.fiscal_periods WHERE fiscal_year = 2025
) AND status = 'REVERSED'; -- or any status indicating a refund
```

##### 3. `run_readonly_sql`

Input:
```json
{
  "sql": "SELECT fees.portfolio_id,\n       (SUM(fees.total_advisory_fee_revenue) * 100.0 / SUM(holdings.total_assets_under_management)) AS percentage_revenue\nFROM (SELECT portfolio_id, SUM(revenue) AS total_advisory_fee_revenue\n      FROM ba9_finance.finance.fees\n      WHERE fiscal_period_id IN (SELECT id FROM ba9_finance.finance.fiscal_periods WHERE fiscal_year = 2025)\n      GROUP BY portfolio_id) AS fees\nJOIN (SELECT ba9_finance.finance.holdings.portfolio_id, SUM(ba9_finance.finance.holdings.market_value_cents) AS total_assets_under_management\n      FROM ba9_finance.finance.holdings\n      JOIN ba9_finance.finance.portfolios ON ba9_finance.finance.holdings.portfolio_id = ba9_finance.finance.portfolios.id\n      WHERE ba9_finance.finance.portfolios.branch_id IS NOT NULL\n      AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM ba9_finance.finance.holdings.as_of) = 2025\n      GROUP BY ba9_finance.finance.holdings.portfolio_id) AS holdings\nON fees.portfolio_id = holdings.portfolio_id\nGROUP BY fees.portfolio_id",
  "datasourceId": "eb6ab04b-a8c1-4859-91ff-4f75d7f1d331",
  "limit": 100,
  "rationale": "Now that I have both total advisory fee revenue and total assets under management by portfolio, I can calculate the percentage revenue for fiscal 2025."
}
```

#### Answer

The information about clients' investments is housed in three key entities: **Portfolios**, **Holdings**, and **Securities**. Here's what each of these contains:

### 1. Portfolios
- **Portfolio ID**: Unique identifier for each portfolio.
- **Portfolio Number**: A designated number for identification (e.g., PF-020203).
- **Customer ID**: ID linking back to the customer associated with the portfolio.
- **Branch ID**: ID for the branch managing the portfolio.
- **Mandate**: Type of investment management (e.g., Execution Only, Advisory, Discretionary).
- **Risk Profile**: Investment risk category (e.g., Growth, Balanced, Conservative).
- **Base Currency**: Currency in which the portfolio operates (e.g., USD).
- **Advisory Fee**: Advisory fees charged, in basis points.
- **Opened On**: Date the portfolio was established.
- **Closed On**: Date the portfolio was closed (if applicable).
- **Status**: Indicates whether the portfolio is Open or Closed.
- **Is Test**: Flag indicating if the portfolio is a test account.

### 2. Holdings
- **Holding ID**: Unique identifier for each holding within a portfolio.
- **Portfolio ID**: ID linking back to the associated portfolio.
- **Security ID**: ID for the security in the holding.
- **As Of**: Date of the record.
- **Quantity**: Amount of the security held.
- **Market Value**: Current market value of the holding, expressed in cents.
- **Cost Basis**: Cost of the holding, also expressed in cents (nullable).

### 3. Securities
- **Security ID**: Unique identifier for each security.
- **Symbol**: Trading symbol for the security (e.g., NWSC).
- **Name**: Full name of the security (e.g., Northwind Semiconductors).
- **Asset Class**: Type of asset (e.g., Equity, Bond, Fund, Cash).
- **Sector**: Sector in which the security operates (nullable).

This structure allows tracking of individual investor portfolios, their corresponding holdings, and the characteristics of the securities they are invested in. Each investment is linked to a specific client, providing a comprehensive view of client investments.

Let me know if you need further details on any specific aspect!

1. **ID**: A unique identifier for each card (e.g., 1, 2, 3).
2. **Card Number (Masked)**: The masked number of the card (e.g., "4929 **** **** 6081").
3. **Account ID**: A reference to the associated account (e.g., links to account IDs like 3, 4).
4. **Network**: The card network (e.g., "VISA", "MASTERCARD").
5. **Card Type**: Indicates whether the card is a "DEBIT" or "CREDIT".
6. **Issued On**: The date the card was issued (e.g., "2019-08-02").
7. **Expires On**: The expiration date of the card (e.g., "2023-08-31").
8. **Status**: The current status of the card (e.g., "ACTIVE", "EXPIRED", "CANCELLED").

This information collectively helps in managing and tracking card-related data effectively.
